## Me
`enade_dataset.tsv` lists all the documents in the `filename` column (please consider just the stem). `enade_c4.md` shows the output of the `grep` command searching occurrences of `percepção sobre a prova` and `percepção da prova`. Confirm if a chapter named `percepção sobre a prova` or `percepção da prova` (case-insensitive) is present in all the documents.

## AI Assistant
No — a chapter named **“percepção sobre a prova”** or **“percepção da prova”** is **not present in all documents** listed in `enade_dataset.tsv` when considering only the filename stem.

From `enade_c4.md`, many documents do have matching chapter/header occurrences, e.g.:

- 2009 documents commonly have **“Capítulo 4: Percepção sobre a Prova”**
- 2010+ documents commonly have **“Capítulo 4: Percepção da Prova”**
- 2022/2023 documents often have **“CAPÍTULO 5/3 PERCEPÇÃO DA PROVA”**

However, at least one dataset document is clearly missing from the grep results:

- `enade_2015_ling_por`

And its content shows chapters about **Língua Portuguesa** performance, not a chapter titled **Percepção da Prova** or **Percepção sobre a Prova**.

So the confirmation is:

> **No, the chapter is not present in all documents.**  
> The grep output shows the chapter in many documents, but not in every filename stem from `enade_dataset.tsv`; `enade_2015_ling_por` is a clear counterexample.
